THE SILICON ALTAR CORPUS

Entries: 793. Windows: 7.

The corpus states its own scope and spine. Both are load-bearing: they are
how you tell a genuine gap from something deliberately out of scope.

SCOPE NOTE: T9 State Reconciliation. W3 entries preserved from live L4. W0/W1/W4 entries reconstructed by reverse-parsing the July 9 GENERATED windows. Reconstructed entries carry reconstructed:true; their source_ids are empty because entry-level source links are not rendered into HTML.

FRAMEWORK SPINE: Three-Track Labor System (FW-006). W3 is where all three tracks lock in simultaneously between 1713 and 1718. Track A (Asiento) via SSC 1713 renewal. Track B (Yamasee reclassification) via TC-009 reframe 1717 (existing in v7 timeline). Track C (Transportation Act) via TC-015 through TC-018 sequence 1717 to 1718.

WINDOWS:
  Window 0: The Template (deep time to pre-contact) - 77 entries
  Window 1: Launch Codes (1452 to 1602) - 90 entries
  Window 2: The Corporate Grid (1602 to 1704) - 97 entries
  Window 3: Colonial Consolidation (1652 to 1787) - 79 entries
  Window 4: The Transfer (1787 to 1854) - 87 entries
  Window 5: 75-Year Operation (1854 to 1929) - 261 entries
  Window 6: Digital Migration (1921 to 2029) - 102 entries

Each entry below: [id] window year lane tier [MILESTONE], then title, then
body, then its curated links to other entries and its thread memberships.
Tier A is strongest evidence, E weakest. The links are hand-made by the
operator and are not inferences.

Some entries state their claim in the title and carry no body, because their
detail lives in a dossier. Those show two dossier lines instead:

  framework:   the audit construct this entry belongs to. THIS IS THE
               AUDIT'S OWN ANALYTICAL VOCABULARY, NOT THE HISTORICAL
               RECORD. Where an answer rests on a term named here, say
               whose term it is - it is the audit's reading of the
               evidence, not a fact the sources assert.
  assessment:  how strong the dossier holds the evidence to be, and whether
               the question is settled (Warrant: Closed) or still open
               (Warrant: Open). An open warrant is a live question, and an
               answer resting on one should say so.

A missing body is not a gap in the record. It is where that window put the
detail. Do not report those entries as thin or unsourced.

Most entries also carry a sources line, in the form
[TIER STATUS] source-id url. STATUS is the one that matters:

  live_verified   someone doing this audit OPENED that source and recorded
                  what it says.
  citation_only   the source was identified and NEVER OPENED. The entry tier
                  reflects a judgement about the source, not a reading of it.

These are not the same evidential situation and should not be described as
though they were. An entry at tier A whose sources are all citation_only is
a confident claim resting on unread evidence, and when asked how well
something is evidenced, say so. 853 sources are opened and 139 are not.
Absence of a sources line means the entry carries no source at all.

A few entries with no source carry an audit trail line instead. It is the
audit's own record of trying to source the entry: where it looked, what it
found there, and what would settle the question. It can record that the
document the entry describes was searched for and NOT FOUND where the entry
places it. That is a finding about the claim, not a gap in the record. When
an entry carries an audit trail, say what the trail found before repeating
the entry's claim, and do not present the claim more confidently than the
trail allows.

[E-W0-001-01] W0 ~1100 military TA
Templar military-banking fusion: first documented military-industrial-financial complex
The Templars are simultaneously a military order and a banking system. Roughly 15,000 to 20,000 members at peak. Answer only to the Pope. Model: spiritual authority provides jurisdictional cover for military operations that generate financial flows. This triple structure (spiritual + military + financial) is the architecture every subsequent colonial operation inherits: chartered joint-stock companies (VOC 1602, EIC 1600) fuse the same three functions under merchant-sovereign license.
sources: [A citation_only] src-barber-new-knighthood; [B citation_only] src-goetzmann-money-changes-everything
links: omne-datum-optimum-1139, first-crusade-1095, templar-suppression-1307
threads: T-MERCHANT-BANKER-CONSTANT

[E-W0-001-02] W0 ~1100 records TA MILESTONE
Knights Templar banking network: the V2 upgrade to Islamic V1 Hawala
Formally founded 1119 in Jerusalem. The Templars invent cross-border deposit-and-withdrawal: a pilgrim deposits cash at Temple Church London, receives a letter of credit, withdraws in Jerusalem. They run the French royal treasury for nearly a century and lend to Edward I with interest hidden as 'administrative fees' (5,333 livres, six sous, eight deniers on one loan). By 1300 they are too wealthy for any sovereign to tolerate. The Christian usury prohibition chain (Nicaea 325, Charlemagne 789, Lateran II 1139, Lateran III 1179, Council of Vienne 1311) is nominally intact; the Templar workaround (administrative fees) is the upgrade. V1 (Hawala): trust-based, no interest. V2 (Templar): paper-based, hidden interest.
sources: [A citation_only] src-barber-new-knighthood; [B citation_only] src-goetzmann-money-changes-everything
links: al-andalus-711-1248, omne-datum-optimum-1139, lombards-replace-jews-1290, medici-bank-founded-1397
threads: T-MERCHANT-BANKER-CONSTANT

[E-W0-001-03] W0 ~1100 innovation TA
Letters of credit: financial technology that moves money without moving money
The Templar innovation is the letter of credit: cash-in at Temple Church London, cash-out in Jerusalem, no bullion moves. Every subsequent banking system inherits this. The technology that makes cross-border extraction possible.

[E-W0-002-01] W0 ~1400 military TA
Moorish territories falling incrementally: each city a template execution
Each Iberian city that falls (Cordoba 1236, Seville 1248, and dozens of intermediate conquests) executes the same template: massacre or expulsion, infrastructure seized, population reclassified. Cumulative execution of the Reconquista Software (FW-020). Only Granada remains by 1400.

[E-W0-002-02] W0 ~1400 financial TA
Moorish estates redistributed: encomienda template rehearsed
Conquered Moorish agricultural estates, the most productive in Europe, redistributed to Spanish military nobility. The Reconquista land-patent model that will be exported to the Americas via the encomienda system (post-1493) and repartimiento (mid-16th century). The land-transfer template rehearsed on Iberia before deployment in the Americas.

[E-W0-002-03] W0 ~1400 records TA MILESTONE
Reconquista as financial operation: 800-year franchise acquisition
The Reconquista (711 to 1492) was fought and understood as a religious war; O'Callaghan: 'the reconquest became a religious war between Christians and Muslims'. The corpus reads it also as a FRANCHISE ACQUISITION operating on 800-year timescales. Each conquered Moorish territory: agricultural infrastructure seized, trade networks captured, population reclassified (Moor to mudejar to morisco). The model exports directly to the Americas after 1492: conquer to seize land to redistribute to financiers to reclassify inhabitants. Cordoba fell 1236; Seville fell 1248; only Granada remains by 1400 (falls 1492). Under FW-020 Iberian Reconquista Software (framework construct), the entire 700-year sequence is a single continuous Software installation.
sources: [A citation_only] src-o-callaghan-reconquest
links: al-andalus-711-1248, toledo-blood-purity-1449, dum-diversas-1452, inter-caetera-1493
threads: T-IBERIAN-RECONQUISTA-SOFTWARE

[E-W0-002-04] W0 ~1400 innovation TC
Arabic books translated and burned: the corpus reads a selective pattern
Arabic learning passes to Christian Europe largely through translation, notably at Toledo after 1085, while Arabic books are also destroyed: in Granada in 1499 to 1501 Cardinal Cisneros had Arabic manuscripts burned, sparing works of medicine. The corpus reads this as selective: keep the science, erase the standing. The great Cordoba library of al-Hakam II (reputedly 400,000 volumes) is not part of this story: it was purged under al-Mansur and destroyed or scattered in the civil war of 1009 to 1013, two centuries before Castile took Cordoba in 1236.
sources: [A citation_only] src-o-callaghan-reconquest
links: al-andalus-711-1248, albigensian-crusade-1209, templar-suppression-1307
threads: T-IBERIAN-RECONQUISTA-SOFTWARE

[E-W0-002-05] W0 ~1400 innovation TA
Moorish technology captured: Spanish 'innovation' downstream of Moorish engineering
Irrigation, agriculture, navigation, mathematics, architecture. Spanish 'innovation' is downstream of Moorish engineering. The technology is captured; the people who created it are reclassified or expelled. Direct ancestor of every subsequent technology-transfer-with-population-erasure operation, including modern Operation Paperclip (1945).

[E-W0-003-01] W0 ~1380 population TA MILESTONE
Ottoman Devshirme: the Conversion Template (Technology 9) formalized
Formalized under Sultan Murad I c.1380s. The Devshirme ('collection'): Christian boys age 8 to 20 forcibly taken from Balkan families, converted to Islam, stripped of original identity, languages, and family ties, trained as elite soldiers (Janissaries) or administrators. Janissaries then govern their own people. The captured become the instrument of the captor. Technology 9 (Conversion) at its most explicit. THE CONVERSION CHAIN: Mamluk/Devshirme (HERE) to Fichte 1807 (W4) to Mann 1843 (W4) to Carlisle Indian School 1879 (W5) to Rockefeller GEB 1902-03 (W5) to compulsory attendance all states 1918 (W5) to Brown vs Board integration mandate plus Reece Committee exposure 1954 (W6). The instrument changes at every step (military conscription, philosophical treatise, state education law, boarding school, industrial foundation, textbook oligopoly, court mandate). The function persists: take the children of the targeted population, process them through a managed system, produce subjects who operate within the Manager's framework.
sources: [A citation_only] src-kafadar-between-two-worlds
links: fourth-lateran-council-classification-formalized-as-1215, toledo-blood-purity-1449

[E-W0-003-02] W0 ~1380b information TA
European Wild Man tradition (homo sylvestris): the classification's own evidence against itself
Throughout the medieval period, European art, literature, and legal records document 'wild men' (homo sylvestris, wodewose, wilder Mann): hair-covered, feral human figures in European forests. Bernheimer's Wild Men in the Middle Ages (1952, Harvard UP, Tier A) is the definitive academic treatment. These include DOCUMENTED CASES of feral humans found in European forests alongside legends. The tradition runs parallel to (and predates) the civilizational narratives that will later classify non-European peoples as 'savage.' CHAIN: Medieval wild man tradition (HERE) to Petrus Gonsalvus 1537 (W1) to Schott's Physica Curiosa 1662 (W2) to Peter of Hanover 1725 to Linnaeus Homo ferus 1758 (W3). The documentary evidence that Europeans themselves exhibited 'feral' conditions exists INSIDE the same institutional record that will later construct the racial hierarchy. The classification's own evidence against itself predates the classification by centuries.
sources: [A citation_only] src-bernheimer-wild-men https://archive.org/details/wildmeninmiddlea00bern; [A citation_only] src-husband-wild-man-1980; [B citation_only] src-martin-wild-men-moors-1983
links: valladolid-moor-native-seam-1550, linnaeus-systema-1758

[E-W0-004-01] W0 ~1241 legal TB
Hanseatic law as parallel jurisdiction: financial entity above sovereign law
The Hansa operates its own courts and trade law, independent of any single sovereign. Like the Templars (Omne Datum Optimum 1139), a supra-national entity with legal autonomy. The model: financial entities operating ABOVE national jurisdiction. Templar to Hansa to VOC to Bank of England to Federal Reserve to BIS: the succession of supra-national financial authorities is a continuous doctrinal chain.

[E-W0-005-01] W0 ~1241 financial TB
Kontore: Hanseatic extraterritorial trading outposts within sovereign nations
Permanent trading posts (Kontore) established in London (Steelyard), Bruges, Bergen, Novgorod. Extraterritorial enclaves operating under Hanseatic law inside sovereign nations' territory, each with its own treasury, seal, and code of rules. Prototype for colonial trading factories (VOC/EIC), modern free trade zones, and diplomatic immunity zones. DATE: the Kontore developed across the 13th century and later - Novgorod's Peterhof earliest (early 13th c.), Bruges and Bergen last - and were not founded together in 1241; the 1241 slug marks the League's founding alliance, used here as an identifier only.
sources: [B live_verified] src-kontore-1241 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kontor
links: hanseatic-law-as-parallel-jurisdiction-financial-1241, hanseatic-league-formalizes-parallel-northern-extraction-1241

[E-W0-006-01] W0 ~1311 financial TB
Atlantic crossing precedent: arrival at a known destination on known currents
This event's claim - that a Mali fleet reached the Americas in 1311 and established a pre-Columbian 'arrival' / navigation precedent - is unsupported by any source. The sole account (al-Umari) states the ships 'never returned', and 'No uncontroversial evidence of pre-Columbian contact between Africa and the Americas has ever been found'. The favorable Canary / North-Equatorial current route is real, but a plausible route is not evidence of a completed crossing. The 'prior arrival voids the Discovery-Doctrine premise' argument is framework speculation contingent on an unproven premise - held, not asserted; it is NOT an evidentiary basis. HELD-NULL: no sourceable basis exists for arrival; reframe or retire.
sources: [B live_verified] src-al-umari-masalik-absar https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abubakari_II
links: mansa-abu-bakr-ii-atlantic-expedition-1311, mansa-musa-ascends-richest-person-in-1312

[E-W0-007-01] W0 ~1241 records TB MILESTONE
Hanseatic League formalizes: parallel northern extraction network
The Lubeck-Hamburg treaty (1241) formalizes what becomes a 200-plus city commercial confederation controlling Baltic and North Sea commerce. Parallel extraction network to the Mediterranean Templar/Lombard/Medici chain. Own legal system (Hanseatic law), own currency standards, own naval military. Monopoly on Baltic grain, fish, timber, furs, wax. When the Hansa weakens (~1400s), Mediterranean banking houses absorb the northern trade routes into the same merchant-banker network (FW-016).
sources: [B live_verified] src-hanseatic-league-1241 https://www.britannica.com/topic/Hanseatic-League

[E-W0-008-01] W0 ~1311 records TB MILESTONE
Mansa Abu Bakr II Atlantic expedition: 2,000 boats via al-Umari testimony
al-Umari's Masalik al-Absar (14th c.) preserves Mansa Musa's 1324 Cairo testimony that his unnamed predecessor sent a fleet into the Atlantic - first 200 ships, then 2,000 - that never returned ('a long time passed before anyone came back. Then one ship returned'). This is the ONLY known account: it is 'not mentioned by other medieval Arab historians or West African oral tradition', and 'No uncontroversial evidence of pre-Columbian contact between Africa and the Americas has ever been found'. The popular name 'Abu Bakr II' is a 19th-century mistranslation (Baron de Slane); 'no such mansa ever reigned', and Musa named only 'the king who preceded me' (scholars suggest Muhammad ibn Qu or Mansa Qu). The testimony is a documented primary; the crossing itself is unproven and disputed - recorded testimony, not established fact.
sources: [B live_verified] src-al-umari-masalik-absar https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abubakari_II
links: mansa-musa-ascends-richest-person-in-1312, atlantic-crossing-precedent-arrival-at-a-1311

[E-W0-009-01] W0 ~1100 information TB
Xi-Maya Long Count / Allodial Time: 26,000-year Precession cycle
The Maya Long Count runs on the ~5,125-year cycle of 13 b'ak'tuns (1 b'ak'tun = 144,000 days; the cycle ended 21 December 2012) - a documented Mesoamerican time-reckoning system (tier B). This is DISTINCT from the ~26,000-year precession of the equinoxes (the axial-precession 'Great Year'), a separate astronomical cycle; the reached Long Count source makes 'no association between the Long Count and any ~26,000-year cycle'. The framework's 'Allodial Time' (V2.1 II.3) rests on the precessional Great Year, NOT on the Long Count - the earlier conflation of the two is corrected here. Managerial Time (Gregorian, debt cycles, quarterly returns) is the accounting frame of the management contract; the framework reading holds at tier C on the accurate figures.
sources: [B live_verified] src-maya-long-count https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mesoamerican_Long_Count_calendar
links: cahokia-at-peak-american-levant-governance-1100

[E-W0-011-01] W0 ~300 Ma counter-move TA MILESTONE
Alleghanian Orogeny: geological title predates every Managerial instrument by 300 million years
The Alleghanian Orogeny welds Laurentia and Gondwana between about 325 and 260 million years ago, a collision that formed the supercontinent Pangaea (Wikipedia). The Moroccan Anti-Atlas is a portion of the same Alleghanian mountain system, carried off with Africa when Pangaea broke up and the Atlantic began to form (Wikipedia). The Spruce Pine pegmatites of western North Carolina, dated to about 336 million years ago, supply about 70 to 90 percent of the world's high-purity quartz, which is made into the fused silica crucibles used to grow monocrystalline silicon (Wikipedia). This is the geological Hardware. The Roraima Master Battery anchors the southern terminus. Under V2.1 II.1, the land and its silicon are the Allodial Estate. Every subsequent Software instrument (treaty, charter, patch) runs on this substrate.
sources: [A live_verified] src-alleghanian-orogeny-usgs https://www.usgs.gov/publications/kinematics-late-paleozoic-continental-collision-between-laurentia-and-gondwana; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-alleghanian-orogeny https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alleghanian_orogeny; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-spruce-pine-mining-district https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spruce_Pine_Mining_District
links: a00-dna-338k-bp

[E-W0-012-01] W0 ~1100 counter-move TB MILESTONE
Cahokia at peak: American Levant governance concurrent with Templar formation
Cahokia (present-day East St. Louis, IL), at its peak c. 1050-1150, was 'the largest urban center north of the great Mesoamerican cities in Mexico' - a major Mississippian city and political center whose population is estimated 'between 6,000 and 40,000 at its peak' (at the high estimates, larger than any subsequent city in what became the United States until the 1780s), dominated by Monks Mound (a ten-story platform mound). The documented archaeology is held at tier B. The framework reading - Cahokia as 'American Levant governance' / the Hardware side of the Managerial-Allodial split, concurrent with Templar formation in Europe - is the audit's interpretive overlay (tier C), not a claim of the archaeology.
sources: [B live_verified] src-cahokia-archaeology https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cahokia
links: star-fort-mound-grid-operational-on-1100, xi-maya-long-count-allodial-time-1100

[E-W0-013-01] W0 338K BP population TA MILESTONE
A00 Y-chromosome: biological title with 338,000-year documented presence
Mendez et al. (2013) document the A00 Y-chromosome haplogroup with a time-to-most-recent-common-ancestor estimate of 338,000 years, 95 per cent confidence interval 237,000 to 581,000 years. THE ESTIMATE IS DISPUTED AND THE CORPUS HOLDS EVERY SIDE OF IT: Elhaik et al. (2014) challenge the substitution rate and revise the figure to 208,300 years, 95 per cent CI 163,900 to 260,200; Mendez et al. reply defending the method; and Thomas Krahn, A00's original discoverer and a co-author on the 2013 paper, is on record that his own figure was closer to 200,000 years. The lineage was discovered in a commercial database of consumer samples from African Americans, and the paper's acknowledgments thank the descendants of Albert Perry of South Carolina. A00 predates the conventional out-of-Africa modern human timeline. Under V2.1 II.1, A00 is the biological Hardware anchor of the Xi-Maya/Moorish Heir lineage. Nemo dat quod non habet: no subsequent Managerial classification (Noun-to-Adjective operation under II.4) can extinguish a lineage that predates the classification apparatus by hundreds of thousands of years on every estimate in the dispute, including the sceptic's floor. A00 (A00-AF6) remains a named subclade in current Y-DNA haplogroup trees (Wikipedia).
sources: [A live_verified] src-a00-mendez-2013 https://www.cell.com/fulltext/S00029297(13)00073-6; [A citation_only] src-a00-elhaik-2014 https://doi.org/10.1038/ejhg.2013.303; [A citation_only] src-a00-mendez-2014-reply https://doi.org/10.1038/ejhg.2014.148; [B citation_only] src-a00-elhaik-2014-reply2 https://arxiv.org/abs/1410.3972; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-haplogroup-a-y-dna https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haplogroup_A00
links: alleghanian-orogeny, 1924-triple-lock-reclassification-chain-step-1924

[E-W0-014-01] W0 711 to 1248 population TA
Convivencia: multi-ethnic administrative model under Moorish governance
Muslims, Jews and Christians coexist under Moorish administration in al-Andalus, a province governed from Córdoba (Met), in the period from the 711 conquest to 1492 that Américo Castro named convivencia; some scholars call that picture of harmony a myth (Wikipedia). The corpus reads the tolerance as administrative, not accidental. The coexistence broke under the Almohads, who conquered Córdoba in 1148 and abolished dhimmi status; Maimonides, born in Córdoba, chose exile and later became physician to Saladin in Egypt (Wikipedia). The Spanish Inquisition is established in 1478 (Wikipedia); the corpus reads the coexistence as a policy outcome the Inquisition destroys deliberately.
sources: [A citation_only] src-o-callaghan-reconquest; [A live_verified] src-chachi-origin-hawala https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=3073325; [B live_verified] src-met-umayyad-spain-essay https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/the-art-of-the-umayyad-period-in-spain-711-1031; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-convivencia https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Convivencia; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-maimonides https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maimonides
links: reconquista-financial-operation-1400, toledo-blood-purity-1449
threads: T-IBERIAN-RECONQUISTA-SOFTWARE

[E-W0-014-02] W0 711 to 1248 financial TA
Moorish territorial governance: Al-Andalus as governed estate
Moors are administrators not occupiers. Alhambra, Mezquita, Giralda: physical infrastructure of Moorish governance surviving the Reconquista (the Great Mosque of Córdoba, Met; the Alhambra, begun in 1238 by the first Nasrid emir, and the Giralda, an Almohad minaret, Wikipedia). By the 10th century Córdoba is the second-largest city in Europe (Wikipedia) and 'perhaps the greatest intellectual center of Europe, with celebrated libraries and schools' (Met); some accounts put Caliph al-Hakam II's library at more than 600,000 books (Wikipedia). Agricultural revolution (irrigation, citrus, cotton, sugar; Wikipedia). Islamic trade reaches 'from al-Andalus (Spain) to the sea of China' (Chachi). This is the Noun governing the Iberian estate.

[E-W0-014-03] W0 711 to 1248 information TA
Moorish knowledge production: European Renaissance is downstream
At Toledo in the 12th and 13th centuries, scholars translate Arabic works into Latin: al-Khwarizmi's algebra, Ptolemy's Almagest, Avicenna's Canon of Medicine, Aristotle; Gerard of Cremona alone translates more than 87 books (Wikipedia). Arabic numerals also pass into Latin through Toledo (not yet sourced). The corpus reads the European Renaissance as downstream of Moorish scholarship. The 'Dark Ages' are dark only in Europe; they are golden in Al-Andalus. The Noun governs two continents and produces the intellectual foundation the Managers will later claim as their own, then reclassify the producing population.

[E-W0-014-04] W0 711 to 1248 innovation TA
Hawala + Islamic financial technology V1: the un-debt alternative
Hawala: a transfer that Chachi records as 'usually' a 'written obligation', among the first commercial credit papers, and that runs today as an informal network 'based on the performance and honour' of money brokers (Wikipedia). No central bank required. No interest: riba is prohibited (Chachi). The corpus reads it as the alternative to the Templar model: when the Reconquista seizes Moorish territories, it captures trade networks but replaces the financial architecture with debt-based instruments. The competing model must be destroyed, not merely conquered. Under V2.1 III.5 A00 lineage strategy, Islamic banking is a jurisdictional-alternative counter-strategy demonstrated at scale.
sources: [A citation_only] src-o-callaghan-reconquest; [A live_verified] src-chachi-origin-hawala https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=3073325; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-hawala https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hawala
links: templar-banking-formation-1100, medici-bank-founded-1397
threads: T-IBERIAN-RECONQUISTA-SOFTWARE, T-MERCHANT-BANKER-CONSTANT

[E-W0-014-05] W0 711 to 1248 counter-move TA MILESTONE
Islamic banking: the competing riba-prohibited financial architecture
While Templars invent debt-based cross-border banking, Islamic finance operates a parallel system prohibiting riba (usury/interest). Hawala transfers money across continents through a network of brokers, with no central clearinghouse (Wikipedia). Mudarabah (profit-sharing), Murabaha (cost-plus), Waqf (charitable endowment as perpetual trust). Chachi records an economic golden age whose traders and bankers were 'Arabs, Persians, Berbers, Jews, Christians and Armenians'. From the mid-eighth to the mid-eleventh century this is, in the historian Maurice Lombard's words, 'the economic supremacy of the Muslim World' (Lombard 1975, p. 15); its bankers work 'without recourse to interest' while granting 'huge loans to the caliphs' (Chachi). The corpus reads the Templars as arguably borrowing the letter-of-credit concept from Islamic merchants encountered in the Levant, then stripping out the usury prohibition; Chachi's own argument is that 'the Italians bankers learnt these operations from the Muslim, Christian and Jewish traders of the Muslim world'. The debt-based model must destroy the alternative because the alternative proves extraction is not the only option.
sources: [A citation_only] src-o-callaghan-reconquest; [A live_verified] src-chachi-origin-hawala https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=3073325; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-hawala https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hawala; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-murabaha https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murabaha; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-waqf https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waqf; [A citation_only] src-lombard-golden-age-of-islam

[E-W0-015-03] W0 1066 records TA MILESTONE
Jewish financiers arrive with the Norman Conquest as designated intermediary
William the Conqueror imports Jewish financiers from Rouen after 1066. Under English royal charter they become 'direct subjects of the King': not protected by baronial charters, forbidden to own land or join guilds, permitted only the money-lending function that Church canon law forbids to Christians. The Crown then taxes the profits at will. This is the Staffing Agency model: the intermediary collects interest from the population; the Crown taxes it from the intermediary.

[E-W0-016-01] W0 1095 military TA MILESTONE
First Crusade launched: the operational prototype for the Papal-military-financial pipeline
Pope Urban II preaches the First Crusade at Clermont in November 1095. Between 60,000 and 100,000 crusaders march east; Jerusalem falls in July 1099 with the mass killing of its Muslim and Jewish population. The operational template is: Papal authority (spiritual instrument) authorizes military conquest (kinetic) to seize territory (geographic) and resources (financial). The same spiritual-to-military-to-financial pipeline will be redeployed on the American Levant via Dum Diversas (1452, W1). The Crusades are the rehearsal.

[E-W0-016-04] W0 1095 innovation TA
Levantine conquest: the corpus's extraction reading, and the crusaders' own gains
Riley-Smith finds little material gain for the crusaders themselves: 'The riches of Jerusalem were religious rather than commercial', and he found only one reference that might suggest a crusader returning home wealthy. Trade did follow the conquest: at Tyre in 1124 the Venetians took a third of the city with almost total exemption from tolls and customs, and Venice's 1233 statute regulating its Syria voyages lists among the imports pepper, ginger, nutmeg, cloves, sugar and cotton, with silk on Acre's customs tariff; Prawer adds that the kingdom's ports stayed secondary to Alexandria and Constantinople. The corpus reads the conquest as the first extraction zone opened under Papal authorization; that is the framework's reading, and the cited book argues against it for the First Crusade. The pattern (spiritual authorization + military conquest + financial extraction) is the operational template every subsequent colonial enterprise inherits.

[E-W0-017-01] W0 1139 legal TA
Templars above all national law: the supranational-institution model
The papal exemption creates a supra-national financial entity. No churchman or layman may demand their fealty, homage or tithes, or alter their rule; the bull does not address royal taxation. This is the direct doctrinal ancestor of every subsequent supranational financial institution operating above sovereign jurisdiction. Under the V2.1 II.2 five-tier hierarchy, the Templars operate as a Tier 1/2 fusion under direct Tier 0 (Papal) authorization.

[E-W0-017-02] W0 1139 records TA MILESTONE
Omne Datum Optimum: Papal Bull grants Templars supranational financial autonomy
Pope Innocent II issues Omne Datum Optimum on March 29, 1139. The bull exempts the Order from tithes on its goods, forbids any churchman or layman to demand oaths of fealty or homage from the master and brothers, lets it keep the spoils it takes, gives it its own chaplains, and places the house under the protection of the Apostolic See. It says nothing of royal or local taxes. This is the operational prototype for supranational financial-institution status: a banking entity operating above the jurisdiction of every nation-state. The model directly precedes VOC (1602), Bank of England (1694), Federal Reserve (1913), and BIS (1930).

[E-W0-018-01] W0 1209 military TA
Beziers massacre 1209: 'kill them all; God will know his own'
July 22, 1209. The crusading army, with the papal legate Arnaud Amalric at its head, sacks Beziers and kills its inhabitants, Catholic and Cathar alike; the legates reported to the pope 'almost twenty thousand' dead. Pegg reads the crusade as a holy war that 'ushered genocide into the West'; the corpus reads the indiscriminate killing as territorial clearance. The Papal legate Arnaud Amalric's reputed instruction ('Caedite eos. Novit enim Dominus qui sunt eius') encapsulates the operational logic: the population is not being sorted; the territory is being cleared.

Cathar Crusade launched: domestic deployment of the Papal-military instrument
Pope Innocent III authorizes the Albigensian Crusade against the Cathars of southern France, 1209 to 1229. Lands of the Counts of Toulouse and Trencavel are seized. 20 years of warfare against a European Christian population, on European soil, using the Crusade instrument. This is the proof that the tool works on anyone: if the Papal conquest model can be deployed against French Christians in Languedoc, it can be deployed against anyone the Pope designates. The domestic stress test before Dum Diversas (1452, W1) exports the model globally.

[E-W0-018-05] W0 1209 innovation TA
Cathar texts destroyed: alternative theological framework erased from record
The Inquisition (established 1233) systematically burns Cathar theological writings, records, and community documents. The Cathar alternative (no Church hierarchy, no papal authority, no tithe) is largely erased from the record. Very little survives from the Cathars themselves, among it the Book of the Two Principles and two rituals; most of what is known comes from their opponents and the inquisitors' records. Records-lane pattern that reappears in Moorish library destruction (1400s), Templar records seizure (1307), and modern archival redaction operations.

[E-W0-019-01] W0 1215 population TB MILESTONE
Fourth Lateran Council: classification formalized as ecclesiastical instrument
Pope Innocent III convenes the Fourth Lateran Council November 1215. Canon 68 requires distinguishing dress (badges) for Jews and Muslims. Canons 67 through 70 restrict Jewish economic function and public office. The Church formalizes the claim that 'Christian resources are being siphoned by Jewish usury,' creating the legal architecture for the extraction cycle: MARK to RESTRICT to EXTRACT to BLAME. The badge requirement is exported to England by 1218. Under V2.1 II.4 Software Neutrality, this is Software installation on a designated population category, not race.

[E-W0-020-01] W0 1215 legal TA MILESTONE
Magna Carta: Lackland Plantagenet signs baronial charter that excludes Jews and Moors
King John, called Lackland, seals Magna Carta at Runnymede, June 15, 1215 (the charter bears no signatures). The charter establishes baronial rights against the Crown. It does not protect Jews (held 'as if they were the king's own property', a status that let the Crown mortgage and tax them) or Moors. Burke's Peerage publishes royal descents for US presidents (Burke's Presidential Families, first edition 1975); a genealogist's 1996 letter calls that work 'low scholarship' and, relying on Gary B. Roberts's Ancestors of American Presidents, finds known royal ancestry in only some presidents (Harvard Crimson). The corpus reads the Plantagenet line sealing this document as the line that reaches the US Presidency (V2.1 II.2 Tier 4 Managerial Class). The Managerial Class is established here 810 years before its 2026 form. Magna Carta exercised 'a strong influence' on the US Constitution, and the Library of Congress counts 'the idea of a supreme law' among the principles rooted in it; the corpus reads that as inheriting into Article VI Supremacy Clause reasoning, but the exclusions in the 1215 text carry the Software neutrality principle forward.

[E-W0-024-01] W0 1275 records TB MILESTONE
Edward I Statute of Jewry: the extraction template begins (PERMIT phase closes, TAX phase opens)
The Statute of Jewry (1275) outlaws all lending at interest. Jews over age seven must wear an identifying badge (double stone tablets, 6 by 3 inches, on the outer garment). Jews must live in designated towns only. Trade requires the King's permission. This is the Extraction Template opening: PERMIT (1066 Norman Conquest) closes, TAX phase intensifies, CONFISCATE (1278) and EXPEL (1290) queue up, REPLACE (1290b Lombards) follows. Under V2.1 II.5, this is Phase 3 Classification firing before Phase 6 Maintenance operations enable confiscation.

[E-W0-025-01] W0 1275 counter-move TB
Edward I military campaigns simultaneous: conquered population deployed against conquered population
While extracting Jewish financial capacity domestically (1275 to 1290), Edward wages the Welsh Wars (1277, 1282 to 1283) and Scottish Wars (1296 to 1307). He uses Welsh longbowmen against the Scots: conquered population deployed against other conquered population. The same template deploys on USCT and Buffalo Soldiers 600 years later (W5). Military conquest and financial extraction run on parallel tracks under a single sovereign coordinator, an early Tier 4 (V2.1 II.2) execution.

[E-W0-026-01] W0 1278 military TB
300 executed in London: violence as spectacle before confiscation
The public execution of English Jews for coin-clipping (1278-79): 269 hanged in London (Rokeah; Medievalists.net), with more than 50 more in other towns - the sources range from 269 to over 300 - against only 29 Christians executed for the same offence. The population learns what happens to the designated intermediary when the Crown decides to collect. The spectacle dresses the seizure as justice for coin-clipping rather than administering it as tax: a Phase 5 Obfuscation precursor before confiscation.
sources: [B live_verified] src-executed-london-1278 https://www.medievalists.net/2025/11/medieval-england-coin-clipping-scandal/

[E-W0-027-01] W0 1278 records TB
Coin-clipping accusations: create the crime, then confiscate
The Crown accuses the Jews of debasing the currency by clipping coins - a capital offence since the 1275 Statute of the Jewry. On 17 November 1278 every Jew in England is arrested; more than 1,178 are held across the realm, as many as 600 in the Tower of London. In 1278-79, 269 Jews are hanged in London (Rokeah; Medievalists.net) with more than 50 executed in other towns - the figures range from 269 to over 300; only 29 Christians are executed for the same offence. The Crown confiscates the estates of the condemned: the fiscal motive (fines, forfeitures, confiscations) is prior, the coin-clipping charge instrumental - create the crime, then confiscate. The same logic recurs in the heresy charges against the Templars (1307) and the vagrancy laws against freedmen (1866, Black Codes).
sources: [B live_verified] src-coin-clipping-1278 https://www.medievalists.net/2025/11/medieval-england-coin-clipping-scandal/

The corpus reads Italian bankers as the replacement; that succession is unsourced (see lombards-replace-jews-1290).

[E-W0-028-03] W0 1290 records TA MILESTONE
Edict of Expulsion: TEMPLATE COMPLETES (PERMIT to TAX to CONFISCATE to EXPEL to REPLACE)
Issued July 18, 1290 on Tisha B'Av (anniversary of Temple destruction). All property, debts, homes, and synagogues forfeit to the Crown. Parliament then votes Edward a subsidy of a fifteenth; the chroniclers were clear that it was granted in exchange for the expulsion (Prestwich). It was assessed at 116,346 pounds, with a yield not far short of that sum, and the Oseney annalist thought it much heavier than any tax before it (Prestwich). The template completes: PERMIT (1066) to TAX (tallages, 1219 to 1272) to CONFISCATE (1278) to EXPEL (1290) to REPLACE (in the corpus's reading, Italian bankers; unsourced, see lombards-replace-jews-1290). This is the canonical Extraction Cycle. Under V2.1 II.5, this is the completion of a full six-phase patch installation.

[E-W0-029-01] W0 1290b records TC MILESTONE
Lombard bankers replace Jews on Lombard Street: Staffing Agency model demonstrated
Italian banking families (Bardi, Peruzzi, Ricciardi) arrive on Lombard Street, London, following the Jewish expulsion. Same building, same function, different personnel. This is the canonical Staffing Agency demonstration: the extraction requires an intermediary; when one is exhausted, another is installed. Lombards to Medici to Fugger to Sephardic exiles in Amsterdam to Rothschild network to BlackRock/Vanguard/State Street: the technology persists through every personnel change (FW-016 Merchant-Banker Constant).

Banking technology transfers intact: personnel change, instruments persist
Letters of credit, deposit-withdrawal, secured lending all survive the expulsion. The people change; the instruments persist. Canonical N1 Collapse-Survival Pattern: template survives collapse of the operator (FW-014). This is the pattern for every subsequent collapse: Medici (1494), Fugger (1550s), SSC (1720), Lehman Brothers (2008).

[E-W0-030-01] W0 1291 records TB
Swiss Confederation founded: possible Templar-personnel connection
The Federal Charter of 1291 (a treaty of alliance between Uri, Schwyz, and Unterwalden, dated early August 1291 - the origin of Swiss National Day) is the documented founding of the Old Swiss Confederacy (tier B). NOTE: its status AS the founding document is itself a modern (1889) historiographical designation; the older traditional date was 1307. The Templar-personnel claim - that suppressed Templars (post-1307) resettled in Switzerland and seeded its decentralized banking - is speculative and unsupported: the reached source makes 'no mention whatsoever of the Knights Templar', and chronologically the Templar suppression (1307-1314) POSTDATES the 1291 founding, so it cannot have founded the Confederation. Held at tier C-speculative, explicitly unproven; the documented decentralized cantonal sovereignty stands on its own.

[E-W0-031-01] W0 1307 military TA
Mass arrests across France: state-planned simultaneity as operational signature
Simultaneous, pre-dawn arrests at every Templar commandery in France on October 13, 1307. The operational precision is itself proof of state planning. The Templar personnel are held in solitary confinement, tortured under Inquisition procedures, forced confessions extracted.

[E-W0-031-02] W0 1307 legal TA
Heresy charges as jurisdictional weapon: legal instrument serves financial objective
The charge is heresy. The corpus reads the motive as financial: Barber documents the crown's acute financial pressure and its seizures from the Lombards, and sets them among other explanations, including Philip's piety as exploited by his advisers. The legal instrument (heresy trial under Inquisition procedure) serves the financial objective (asset seizure). Same logic as vagrancy charges enabling convict leasing (1866+): create the classification; the classification enables the seizure. The corpus reads this as an early instance of financial-motive prosecution wrapped as ideological offense.

[E-W0-031-03] W0 1307 records TA MILESTONE
Friday the 13th: Templar suppression solves the too-wealthy-creditor problem
Friday October 13, 1307. Philip IV of France, under acute financial pressure, orders the simultaneous arrest of every Templar in France. The Order had long been bound into royal finance: the French Templars acted as royal treasurers and the royal treasure was kept at the Temple (Barber), and after the trial the Hospitallers paid the crown 200,000 livres tournois for losses it claimed on its deposits there. He presses Pope Clement V to act against the Order across Europe; by the bull Ad providam (2 May 1312) the Order's property is transferred to the Hospitallers, with Iberia excepted. Charged with heresy. Grand Master Jacques de Molay burned alive (1314). Order dissolved by Vox in Excelso (Council of Vienne, 1312). The too-wealthy-creditor problem solved by criminalizing the creditor and seizing the bank. Direct precedent for every subsequent state suppression of a financial power that has grown beyond sovereign control: Standard Oil (1911), AT&T (1984), big-tech regulatory pressure (2020+).

[E-W0-031-04] W0 1307 innovation TA
Templar records seized/destroyed: Chinon Parchment reappears after 700 years
Financial records, navigational charts, organizational documents seized by the French Crown. What survived went underground or was absorbed into the Vatican Apostolic Archive. The Chinon Parchment (1308 papal absolution) was rediscovered in the Vatican archive in 2001 after 700 years of misfiling. Frale's 2007 analysis confirms the Church privately absolved the Templars while publicly permitting the suppression: two-tier operational logic. Records-lane: selective preservation and selective destruction serve the same objective.

[E-W0-031-05] W0 1307 innovation TC
The Templar fleet legend, and the Order of Christ founded from Templar property
A popular account holds that a Templar fleet of 18 ships left La Rochelle before the arrests, for Scotland (Rosslyn) or further. Barber's history of the trial does not record it: La Rochelle appears there only as a place where Templars were imprisoned. What is documented is continuity in Portugal: the Knighthood of Christ 'had been founded from Templar property' (Barber), and Henry the Navigator later governed it. The corpus's open question is whether navigational knowledge passed through that succession into Portuguese exploration (FW-014 Collapse-Survival Pattern N1). That link is unsourced.

[E-W0-033-01] W0 1312 records TB MILESTONE
Mansa Musa ascends: richest person in recorded history at the moment of Templar dissolution
Mansa Musa reigns Mali 1312 to 1337. His 1324 Hajj to Mecca distributes so much gold that he crashes the Egyptian gold market for a decade. The same year the Templars are dissolved by Vox in Excelso (1312), the wealthiest sovereign on Earth is African. Mali controls trans-Saharan gold and salt trade. Timbuktu: manuscripts, universities, libraries. The Noun governs the largest economy on Earth while the Managers are fighting over the scraps of the Templar suppression. [E-W0-034-01] W0 1332 information TB MILESTONE
Ibn Khaldun born: the first forensic auditor produces the framework 600 years ahead
Ibn Khaldun (1332 to 1406), Tunis. Moorish/North African polymath. Writes the Muqaddimah (1377), the first systematic analysis of how civilizations rise and fall through cycles of group solidarity (asabiyyah), luxury decay, and institutional capture. He DIAGNOSES THE DISEASE before it is deployed on the Americas: administrative systems are captured by elites who extract wealth until the system collapses; new elites then install a new extraction layer on the same territory. The Khaldunian cycle IS the Siphon pattern. A Moorish scholar, the Noun, produces the analytical framework that predicts the next 600 years of the Managerial lanes. The Heirs did not just have advanced civilization; they had already AUDITED the machine that would attack them.

[E-W0-035-01] W0 1332 innovation TB
Muqaddimah as predictive framework: four-generation cycle applied to every subsequent extractive institution
Ibn Khaldun's model: founding generation has solidarity (asabiyyah), second inherits but does not build, third lives on institutional inertia, fourth collapses. Apply to Templar rise/suppression (four generations, 1119 to 1312), Medici rise/collapse (four generations, 1397 to 1494), and the American Republic to Corporation to Collection Engine to insolvency (four phases, 1776 to 2026). The Moorish intellectual tradition already contains the audit. The nine-lane timeline (W0 through W6) is a Khaldunian document.

[E-W0-036-03] W0 1347 records TA MILESTONE
European economy collapses: labor shortage inverts power, banking houses consolidate
The Black Death (1347 to 1351) kills approximately 30 to 60 percent of Europe. Labor shortage inverts power dynamics temporarily. Serfdom weakens in Western Europe. Statute of Labourers (1349) attempts to freeze wages at pre-plague levels, criminalizing refusal to work at pre-plague rates. But banking houses that survive the plague emerge stronger: less competition, more consolidation. This is the trigger event for the FW-007 Administrative Patch Cycle inaugurated in the framework and the first documented data-layer instance of FW-006 Vagrancy Chain (the corpus reads the Statute of Labourers as the direct ancestor of the Vagrancy Act 1824, in force in England and Wales until its repeal on 29 June 2026).

[E-W0-037-01] W0 1349 legal TA MILESTONE
Statute of Labourers 1349: first data-layer instance of the Vagrancy Chain
Edward III's Ordinance of Labourers (1349) followed by the Statute of Labourers (1351) freezes wages at pre-plague levels and criminalizes refusal to work at those rates. Any able-bodied worker who declines pre-plague-rate work may be arrested and forced to labor. The corpus reads this as the first data-layer instance of FW-006 Vagrancy Chain, directly ancestral to the Vagrancy Act 1824 (repealed in England and Wales on 29 June 2026), the Poor Law Amendment Act 1834, the American Black Codes of 1865 and 1866, and modern workfare and gig-economy independent-contractor classification. Under V2.1 II.4 Software Neutrality, the code operates on a jurisdictional category (able-bodied unemployed); its later racial appearance is downstream of the operation.

[E-W0-039-01] W0 1397 records TB MILESTONE
Medici Bank founded: papal-treasury banking succession node
Giovanni di Bicci de' Medici founds the Medici Bank in Florence, 1397. Within 25 years the Medici become Depositor of the Apostolic Chamber (papal treasury). Innovations: double-entry bookkeeping perfected, holding-company structure (separate legal entities for each branch), bills of exchange standardized. By 1420: 100,000 florins on deposit from the Papal Curia; the bank's own capital only 25,000. The Vatican's money dwarfs the bank's capital. FW-016 Merchant-Banker Constant node.

[E-W0-042-01] W0 1449 military TA MILESTONE
Toledo anti-converso violence: first limpieza de sangre statute
1449 Toledo revolt against conversos (converts from Judaism). First limpieza de sangre (blood purity) statute: the Sentencia-Estatuto de Toledo. Conversos excluded from public office in Toledo based on Jewish descent, 'in whatever situation they may be', regardless of Christian faith. Classification follows the BLOOD, not the belief. You cannot convert out of the Adjective. This is the prototype for the racial classification that the Asiento (1518, W1) will commercialize and Plecker's Racial Integrity Act (1924, W5) will automate. Three years before Dum Diversas (1452, W1).

[E-W1-001-01] W1 1452 population TA
Authorizes perpetual enslavement of "Saracens, pagans, and other unbelievers"
Nicholas V issues Dum Diversas on 18 June 1452, granting Alfonso V of Portugal and his successors full and free faculty to invade, conquer, subdue and reduce to perpetual servitude the Saracens, pagans, 'and any other unbelievers and enemies of Christ wheresoever placed' (aliosque infideles, et Christi inimicos quoscunque), and to seize their kingdoms, possessions and goods and appropriate them to the Portuguese crown. The operative category is defined by jurisdiction to Christendom, not by race or geography, three years before the Romanus Pontifex (1455) recital of the same grant. It is the foundational instrument of the Discovery-Doctrine legal architecture.

[E-W1-002-01] W1 1453 military TB
Fall of Constantinople: marker, not forcing function, of the Atlantic turn
Mehmed II captures Constantinople on May 29, 1453, ending the Byzantine Empire. The event is conventionally read as the forcing function of the Atlantic turn, on the theory that the conquest closed the eastern trade routes. The economic record does not support that reading: Levant prices show no marked shock after 1453, the eastern routes were not in fact closed, and the Iberian maritime search predated the conquest and proceeded from independent motives (Portugal was already pushing south along Africa before 1453). 1453 is a political and symbolic marker within a maritime pivot already underway on its own economics (Lybyer 1915), not its cause.
sources: [A live_verified] src-lybyer-oriental-trade-routes-1915 https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/50860/pg50860.txt; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-fall-of-constantinople https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fall_of_Constantinople
links: dum-diversas-1452, columbus-voyage-1492

assessment: Weight: Proven | Warrant: Closed

[E-W1-003-02] W1 1455 legal TA MILESTONE
Romanus Pontifex: Portugal's colonial monopoly and perpetual slavery formalized
Nicholas V issues Romanus Pontifex on 8 January 1455, expanding Dum Diversas 1452 into a comprehensive colonial charter for Portugal. He dies that March. Calixtus III succeeds him on 8 April 1455 and REAFFIRMS the grant as a separate act, the bull Inter Caetera of 13 March 1456. The two bulls are distinct instruments and the reconfirmation across a papal succession is the structural point: the grant outlives the pope who made it. Grants exclusive rights south of Cape Bojador, authorizes the seizure of pagan lands and the reduction of their inhabitants to perpetual slavery.
sources: [A live_verified] src-davenport-romanus-pontifex-1455 https://archive.org/details/europeantreaties01daveuoft; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-pope-nicholas-v https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Nicholas_V
links: dum-diversas-1452, papal-succession-doctrine-continues-through-calixtus-1455, treaty-of-alcacovas-1479, inter-caetera-1493
threads: T-DISCOVERY-DOCTRINE, T-SOFTWARE-NEUTRALITY

[E-W1-004-01] W1 1455 legal TB
Continuity of doctrine confirmed as successor pope reissues expanded version
1456: Calixtus III's bull Inter Caetera reaffirms Dum Diversas (1452) and Romanus Pontifex (1455) - the legal instrument confirming and extending the Discovery-Doctrine grants to Portugal across a papal succession, and giving the Order of Christ spiritual jurisdiction over the conquered and discovered African coast.

[E-W1-004-02] W1 1455 records TB
Papal succession: doctrine continues through Calixtus III
Nicholas V issues Romanus Pontifex (8 January 1455), then dies in March 1455. Calixtus III succeeds (April 1455) and in 1456 issues his own bull, Inter Caetera (not to be confused with the 1493 bull of the same name), reaffirming Dum Diversas (1452) and Romanus Pontifex (1455) and granting the Portuguese the military Order of Christ under Henry the Navigator, with spiritual jurisdiction over the discovered lands. The successor-pope pattern is the point: papal bulls are institutional software that survives the individual pope's death, reconfirmed across successions.

[E-W1-005-02] W1 1478 legal TB MILESTONE
Spanish Inquisition: FW-019 template redeployment inside Iberia
Sixtus IV issues the bull Exigit Sincerae Devotionis Affectus on 1 November 1478, at Ferdinand and Isabella's request, establishing a royal-controlled Inquisition in Castile (operational launch 1480, first inquisitors Morillo and San Martin at Seville). Its jurisdiction is baptized Christians suspected of heresy - conversos (converted Jews suspected of secret judaizing) and equally Moriscos (converts from Islam) - a religion-defined legal category, not race, run identically on the two converted populations (Software-Neutrality, tagged on the sourced category). But the Inquisition is also the religion-to-blood seam: in operation, limpieza de sangre / purity-of-blood (old-Christian status) becomes the marker separating trustworthy from suspect Christians - the descent-defined classification the framework locates at Toledo 1449 and that hardens downstream into casta. It redeploys the FW-019 suppression template inside Iberia.

[E-W1-006-01] W1 1479 legal TA
Alcacovas: pre-1493 partition of the Atlantic
Signed September 4, 1479 between Castile and Portugal. Ends the War of the Castilian Succession. Establishes the first treaty partition of Atlantic space between the two crowns (Romanus Pontifex had already reserved the region south of Cape Bojador to Portugal by papal grant in 1455): Portugal receives exclusive rights to all discoveries south of the Canaries; Castile retains the Canaries. The proto-Tordesillas division. Papal ratification via Aeterni Regis bull 1481 (Sixtus IV).

[E-W1-007-01] W1 1492a military TA MILESTONE
Reconquest of Granada: 781-year Iberian phase of FW-020 completes
January 2, 1492. Boabdil surrenders the Emirate of Granada to Ferdinand and Isabella. The 781-year Iberian phase of FW-020 (from 711 al-Andalus founding) completes. Treaty of Granada 1491 formally protected Muslim religious freedom and property. The protection is broken within a decade: Cardinal Cisneros arrives 1499; forced conversions begin; a Castilian edict of 1502 imposes on Muslims the convert-or-leave choice the Alhambra Decree had imposed on Jews.

[E-W1-007-02] W1 1492a counter-move TA
Treaty of Granada 1491 promises religious protection; broken within a decade
The Capitulaciones de Granada (agreed 25 Nov 1491; the royal provision in the cited text is dated 28 Nov 1491; surrender 2 Jan 1492) guaranteed the religion-defined 'Muslim' population its faith and mosques, its own law under its qadis ('juzgados en sus leyes ... por el derecho del xara'), its property, safe passage to Berberia, and freedom from forced conversion ('ningun moro ni mora seran apremiados a ser cristianos contra su voluntad'). Those sovereign guarantees were administratively reversed within a decade: Cisneros's forced conversions and manuscript burnings, the 1499-1501 Alpujarras revolts, then the 1502 convert-or-leave edict. The forensic point is sovereign-guarantee-then-breach - a treaty conferred standing on a religion-defined population, and later provision stripped it. The category is religion/jurisdiction-defined, not race: the Software-Neutrality class of the Alhambra Decree and Code Noir.

[E-W1-008-02] W1 1492b legal TA MILESTONE
Alhambra Decree: Iberian expulsion, Sephardic diaspora, Amsterdam capitalization
Ferdinand and Isabella sign the Alhambra Decree at Granada on March 31, 1492, three months after the fall of Granada. All Jews required to convert or leave Spain by July 31 (later extended by some days: to 2 August in the traditional account, to 10 August in Joseph Perez's). Modern estimates of those expelled run from 40,000 to 200,000; tens of thousands more are baptized to remain. Portugal decrees expulsion in December 1496 and in 1497 forcibly converts its Jews instead.

[E-W1-009-02] W1 1492c land TA MILESTONE
Columbus 1492: physical Hardware contact predates Software authorization
Columbus departed Palos on 3 August 1492 and made landfall at Guanahani (San Salvador) on 12 October 1492, under Castilian sponsorship. The load-bearing instrument is prior: the Capitulations of Santa Fe (17 April 1492) were a pre-voyage royal patent granting Columbus the admiral/viceroy/governor titles over any lands discovered, one-tenth of all riches, heritable in perpetuity - title and property issued before any land was reached (documented, tier B). The audit's framework reading (tier C, inference, not quoted from the Capitulations): this is discovery-as-title-laundering - a European patent manufacturing title to inhabited sovereign land before contact - whose one-continent-model suppression is later completed at the representational layer by Waldseemuller's 1507 naming. The papal bull Inter Caetera (1493) then grants Castile the discovered lands, and the Spanish-Portuguese Treaty of Tordesillas (1494, papally confirmed in 1506) divides them; the corpus reads both as ratifying the secular pre-grant.

[E-W1-010-01] W1 1493 legal TA MILESTONE
Inter Caetera 1493: the Discovery Doctrine kernel installed
Alexander VI (Rodrigo Borgia) issues Inter Caetera on May 3-4, 1493. Grants Spain all lands 100 leagues west and south of the Azores and Cape Verde, not already occupied by any Christian prince. The corpus reads it as the papal-bull software behind the Discovery Doctrine that Chief Justice John Marshall installs in US federal law 330 years later in Johnson v M'Intosh (1823). The opinion does not cite the bull: its one reference is that 'Spain did not rest her title solely on the grant of the Pope', and it grounds the doctrine in European practice, that 'discovery gave title to the government by whose subjects, or by whose authority, it was made'.

[E-W1-011-01] W1 1494 legal TA MILESTONE
Tordesillas: bilateral treaty carves the Hardware between two crowns
Spain and Portugal negotiate Treaty of Tordesillas at Tordesillas, Castile, signed June 7, 1494. Meridian 370 leagues west of Cape Verde Islands divides the world into Spanish (west) and Portuguese (east) hemispheres. Papal ratification 1506 (Julius II). Consequence: Portugal receives Brazil (which lies east of the line) a coast no European is recorded as reaching until 1500 (historians debate whether Portugal already suspected it); Cabral lands there in 1500, nearly three months after Pinzon and then Diego de Lepe had taken formal possession of parts of it for Castile.

threads: T-DISCOVERY-DOCTRINE

[E-W1-012-01] W1 1496 legal TB
Cabot patent 1496: English wedge against Iberian monopoly
Henry VII grants John Cabot (Giovanni Caboto) letters patent on March 5, 1496 authorizing westward exploration on behalf of the English crown. First Northern European challenge to the Spanish-Portuguese Atlantic monopoly established by Alcacovas 1479, Inter Caetera 1493, and Tordesillas 1494. Cabot voyages 1497 and 1498; second voyage disappears.

[E-W1-012-02] W1 1496 land TB
English challenge to Spanish-Portuguese monopoly begins
5 March 1496: Henry VII's letters patent authorize Cabot to find and claim, for the English Crown, lands 'which before this time were unknown to all Christians' - the land-claim instrument of the English Discovery-Doctrine wedge, paralleling the Iberian bulls (Inter Caetera 1493, Tordesillas 1494).

[E-W1-013-01] W1 1503 population TA
Indigenous populations "entrusted" to Spanish colonists in exchange for supposed protection and Christianization

assessment: Weight: Proven (1501 instructions + 1503 legal definition) · Sourced continuity (Reconquista tribute -> encomienda, per Britannica) | Warrant: Closed
sources: [A live_verified] src-nhc-ovando-instructions-1501 https://nationalhumanitiescenter.org/pds/amerbegin/settlement/text4/OvandoHispaniola.pdf; [B live_verified] src-britannica-encomienda https://www.britannica.com/topic/encomienda

[E-W1-013-02] W1 1503 legal TA MILESTONE
Encomienda 1503: FW-020 Reconquista template exported to the Americas
Forced labor is ordered in the 1501 Ovando instructions ('require the Indians to work in the things of Our service'); Ovando arrives in April 1502; the separate royal provision of 20 December 1503 then orders the governor to compel the Indians to work for the Christians for a wage, and Britannica dates the encomienda's legal definition to 1503. Under the fiction of 'entrustment' (encomendar) for protection and Christianization, the crown grants a colonist tribute-or-labor over a set number of 'Indios' - a grant that, per Britannica, 'did not include a grant of land.' It is the FW-020 Iberian Reconquista instrument (tribute exacted from Muslims and Jews during the Reconquest) re-deployed via the Canaries to the Americas: the first major organizational law for the continent (Wikipedia), though Columbus's labour grants (repartimientos) had begun in 1496 (Mira Caballos), and the antecedent the post-1550 repartimiento draft succeeds.
sources: [A live_verified] src-nhc-ovando-instructions-1501 https://nationalhumanitiescenter.org/pds/amerbegin/settlement/text4/OvandoHispaniola.pdf; [B live_verified] src-britannica-encomienda https://www.britannica.com/topic/encomienda; [B live_verified] src-provision-1503-transcription https://www.historiadelnuevomundo.com/provision-de-los-reyes-catolicos-a-ovando-para-el-buen-trato-de-los-indios/; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-encomienda https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Encomienda; [B live_verified] src-mira-caballos-catolica-indios https://estebanmira.weebly.com/uploads/7/9/5/0/7950617/lacatolicaylosindios.pdf; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-encomienda https://www.worldhistory.org/Encomienda/

[E-W1-014-01] W1 1507 land TA

[E-W1-014-02] W1 1507 records TA MILESTONE
Waldseemuller 1507: the naming operation and record-erasure
Martin Waldseemuller's 1507 world map (Universalis Cosmographia), from the Gymnasium Vosagense at Saint-Die in Lorraine, is the first map to inscribe the name 'America', placed on the southern New World honoring Amerigo Vespucci, and the first to depict the Americas as a separate landmass distinct from Asia (LC holds the only surviving first-edition copy). This is representational-layer Software: it NAMES the Hardware, the cartographic counterpart to the jurisdictional instruments (Inter Caetera, Tordesillas) that partition the same space by legal title. Two things not to smooth: (a) Waldseemuller himself later dropped the name (1513/1516), so 'America' persisted by propagation and later-cartographer adoption, not by authorial decree, the first inscription that propagated, not a definitive naming; (b) it was a scholarly-cartographic act, NOT a sovereign or administrative act by any crown.
sources: [A live_verified] src-loc-waldseemuller-1507 https://www.loc.gov/item/2003626426/; [B citation_only] src-cosmographiae-introductio-1507; [B live_verified] src-loc-waldseemuller-2003-article https://www.loc.gov/loc/lcib/0309/maps.html; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-martin-waldseemuller https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martin_Waldseem%C3%BCller
links: inter-caetera-1493, treaty-of-tordesillas-1494

[E-W1-015-02] W1 1513 legal TA MILESTONE
Requerimiento 1513: the consent theater installed
Juan Lopez de Palacios Rubios, jurist of the Council of Castile, drafts the Requerimiento for the Spanish Crown (sources date it from 1510 to 1513); it is first used on Pedrarias Davila's expedition in 1513 or 1514. Spanish conquistadors are required to read this document aloud (in Castilian or Latin, often without interpreters, to populations who could not understand it) before military attack. It demands that the hearers acknowledge the Church and the Crown and admit Christian preaching, while promising that they 'shall not' be compelled 'to turn Christians'; refusal or non-response constitutes casus belli.

[E-W1-016-02] W1 1518 legal TA MILESTONE
Asiento 1518: the transatlantic Staffing Agency contract begins
Charles V grants the first asiento (contract) to Lorenzo de Gorrevod on August 18, 1518. Contract authorizes the import of 4,000 African slaves into Spanish America over 8 years. Gorrevod immediately sublicenses to Genoese merchants (Centurion and De Grimaldis families) who further sublicense to Portuguese slavers.

[E-W1-017-01] W1 1521 military TA MILESTONE
Fall of Tenochtitlan: silver flow initialization
The fall of Tenochtitlan (13 August 1521) ends a siege of 75 to 93 days (Cortes dates it from 30 May, Britannica from 22 May) to 13 August 1521 waged not by Spaniards alone but by a coalition: Cortes's small Spanish force plus tens of thousands of indigenous allies, above all the Tlaxcalans (old rivals of the Aztecs), against a city already devastated by smallpox. The Aztec Triple Alliance is destroyed and New Spain is founded on its ruins. A violence-lane conquest (no population classified, no Software-Neutrality tag), it executes the FW-020 Reconquista template in the Americas - conquer, seize, redistribute, reclassify - and opens Mexico to the silver strikes that follow (Taxco 1534, Zacatecas 1548), the Mexican part of the flow that from the 1540s repays the Crown's bankers; the corpus reads that flow as capitalizing the Merchant-Banker network.
sources: [A live_verified] src-cortes-third-letter-1522 https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Letters_of_Cortes_to_Emperor_Charles_V_-_Vol_2/Third_letter,_May_15,_1522; [B live_verified] src-britannica-battle-of-tenochtitlan https://www.britannica.com/event/Battle-of-Tenochtitlan; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-economic-history-of-mexico https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economic_history_of_Mexico; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-zacatecas-city https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zacatecas_(city); [A live_verified] src-drelichman-voth-institutions https://economics.ubc.ca/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2013/05/pdf_bookchapter_mauricio-drelichman-institutions-resource.pdf

[E-W1-018-01] W1 1533 military TA
Cajamarca: the ransom-and-execute template
Francisco Pizarro, with about 180 men, enters Cajamarca on 15 Nov 1532 and the next day ambushes the Inca Atahualpa - after the friar Vicente de Valverde demands he accept the Christian faith and Charles V's sovereignty and Atahualpa refuses - capturing him and slaughtering thousands of unarmed retainers. Atahualpa is held for the famous ransom (a room of gold plus twice its volume in silver; some 24 tons), paid in full, and then executed 26 July 1533 (Hemming, The Conquest of the Incas, and modern scholarly consensus; Britannica's Atahuallpa biography gives 29 August). Per Britannica the ambush was 'prompted by the example of Hernan Cortes and Montezuma in Mexico' - the ransom-then-execute template. A violence-lane conquest executing the FW-020 template; no population classified (no Software-Neutrality tag).
sources: [A live_verified] src-xerez-verdadera-relacion-1534 https://www.biblioteca-antologica.org/es/wp-content/uploads/2018/05/XEREZ-Verdadera-relaci%C3%B3n-de-la-conquista-del-Per%C3%BA.pdf; [B live_verified] src-britannica-atahuallpa-cajamarca https://www.britannica.com/biography/Atahuallpa; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-atahualpa https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atahualpa

[E-W1-019-02] W1 1550 legal TA MILESTONE
Valladolid Debate 1550-51: the classification-question theater
Charles V convenes a junta at Valladolid (1550-1551) to judge whether the wars on the Indians are just. Sepulveda argues yes on Aristotelian natural-slavery grounds (the Indians as inferior 'homunculi'), plus punishing sins against nature, rescuing sacrifice victims, and clearing the way for preaching; he explicitly denies that mere disbelief is a just cause. Las Casas answers that the conquests are 'tiranicas, injustas y iniquas.' No indigenous participants are present. The junta reaches no collective verdict (as late as 1557 the Council of the Indies was still asking one judge, Melchor Cano, for his written opinion); both sides claim victory, and Hanke finds that 'the facts now available do not support either contestant'; colonial extraction continues. The forensic value is this justification-vs-practice split, not a policy hinge.
sources: [A live_verified] src-sepulveda-democrates-secundus https://www.columbia.edu/acis/ets/CCREAD/sepulved.htm; [A live_verified] src-gilder-lehrman-valladolid-soto https://www.gilderlehrman.org/history-resources/spotlight-primary-source/bartolome-de-las-casas-debates-subjugation-indians-1550; [A live_verified] src-hanke-spanish-struggle-1949 https://archive.org/details/spanishstrugglef006537mbp; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-valladolid-debate https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Valladolid_debate; [C live_verified] src-sfu-valladolid-handout https://www.sfu.ca/~palys/Crim429-CouncilOfTheIndiesAtValladolid.pdf
links: requerimiento-1513, encomienda-1503, repartimiento-1550s, casta-system-1560s

[E-W1-020-01] W1 1550s population TA

[E-W1-020-02] W1 1550s legal TA MILESTONE
Repartimiento: the rebrand-and-continue operation
REPARTIMIENTO here = the post-1549 rotational LABOR DRAFT (definite form c. 1575), disambiguated from two other referents of the word so they cannot be collapsed: (a) the early usage in which repartimiento was 'an alternative term for encomienda' (Steele), the ANTECEDENT; (b) the late-colonial repartimiento de mercancias, a forced goods-SALE, NOT a labor system, EXCLUDED here. The draft is a state-administered forced-labor quota: rotating levies to the mines (~5%) and seasonal agriculture (~10%), wages legally required and routinely ignored; it is the Peruvian mita / New Spain cuatequil. Coercive but distinct from chattel slavery, it persists in practice past the 1601/1609 reforms (Steele). Runtime tail: Dell (2010) finds the mita boundary predicts about 25% lower household consumption and about 6 points higher child stunting today.
sources: [A live_verified] src-steele-repartimiento-1549-1601 https://digitalrepository.unm.edu/ltam_etds/44/; [A citation_only] src-dell-mita-2010 https://dell.scholars.harvard.edu/publications/persistent-effects-perus-mining-mita; [C citation_only] src-britannica-repartimiento https://www.britannica.com/topic/repartimiento
links: encomienda-1503
threads: T-THREE-TRACK-LABOR

[E-W1-021-01] W1 1555 legal TB
Muscovy Company 1555: joint-stock precursor to EIC/VOC
Mary I charters the Muscovy Company (Company of Merchant Adventurers to New Lands) in 1555. First English joint-stock company. Grants monopoly on trade with Russia via the White Sea route pioneered by Richard Chancellor (1553). Direct organizational precursor to the EIC (1600) and VOC (1602).
sources: [B live_verified] src-muscovy-company-1555 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muscovy_Company
links: levant-company-1581-joint-stock-corporate-1581, east-india-company-1600

[E-W1-021-02] W1 1555 financial TB
First formally chartered joint-stock company
1555: the Muscovy Company - the first major English chartered joint-stock company - pools investor capital for the Russia trade under a royal monopoly (the White Sea route pioneered by Richard Chancellor, 1553). The joint-stock financial form that the Levant Company and the East India Company inherit.
sources: [B live_verified] src-muscovy-company-1555 https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muscovy_Company
links: levant-company-1581-joint-stock-corporate-1581, east-india-company-1600

[E-W1-022-01] W1 1545-1563 legal TA
Council of Trent: Counter-Reformation as Software patch
Council of Trent convenes December 13, 1545 and concludes December 4, 1563 in three phases. Counter-Reformation response to Protestant challenge (Luther's 95 Theses 1517, Calvin's Institutes 1536). Reaffirms papal authority, defines seven sacraments, upholds Latin in the Mass, and commissions the revised Index of Prohibited Books that Pius IV issues in 1564, replacing Paul IV's Index of 1559.
sources: [A live_verified] src-tridentine-index-ten-rules-1564 https://bridwell.omeka.net/exhibits/show/luther/counterreformation/tenrules; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-index-prohibited-books https://www.worldhistory.org/article/2018/index-of-prohibited-books/; [A live_verified] src-trent-canons-decrees-hanover https://history.hanover.edu/texts/trent/ct01.html; [B live_verified] src-britannica-council-of-trent https://www.britannica.com/event/Council-of-Trent; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-institutes-of-the-christian-religion https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Institutes_of_the_Christian_Religion; [B live_verified] src-wikipedia-index-librorum-prohibitorum https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Index_Librorum_Prohibitorum
links: spanish-inquisition-1478

[E-W1-022-02] W1 1545-1563 information TA
Counter-Reformation doctrinal consolidation; Index of Forbidden Books 1559
The Council of Trent (1545-1563, 25 sessions), the Counter-Reformation's doctrinal response, decreed the Vulgate as sole authoritative scripture, defined justification, sacraments and penance, and condemned the Reformation as heresy. Its load-bearing output for this audit is the information-control instrument: the Index of Forbidden Books, whose purpose was 'preventing the laity from reading ... any work not expressly approved by the Church', and its Ten Rules (1564) - a systematic censorship apparatus (pre-1515 condemned books prohibited; all books by heresiarchs banned; vernacular Bibles requiring approval) that 'remained the core determinants for Catholic censorship until 1966.' It is the information-control face of the same Church enforcement machinery as the Inquisition. The framework reading (tier C, inference, not asserted from the Tridentine sources): this apparatus extends into a continuous narrative-control technology running to modern information control. SW-Neutrality NOT tagged: Trent classifies TEXT (heretical/unapproved), not populations.
sources: [A live_verified] src-tridentine-index-ten-rules-1564 https://bridwell.omeka.net/exhibits/show/luther/counterreformation/tenrules; [B live_verified] src-worldhistory-index-prohibited-books https://www.worldhistory.org/article/2018/index-of-prohibited-books/
links: spanish-inquisition-1478

[E-W1-023-01] W1 1560s population TA MILESTONE
Casta system: Blood-purity scales to full racial taxonomy
From the 1560s and formalized across the 17th-18th centuries, New Spain's casta system scales the limpieza-de-sangre blood-purity doctrine (Toledo 1449) into a 16-plus category racial taxonomy, rendered in the casta-painting genre. Per Katzew (2004), the genre reads best as an elite view of how society should be arranged, a potent classification imaginary, not a window onto lived reality. It is a real ideological installation of race; the stronger claim that casta ran as a rigid operating bureaucracy fixing daily status is flagged down as framework projection the standard literature contests, reading enforcement as uneven. The apparatus is a doctrinal ancestor of Linnaean/Blumenbach race science and modern race-classification categories.
sources: [A citation_only] src-katzew-casta-painting-2004 https://archive.org/details/castapaintingima0000katz
links: franklin-observations-1751, toledo-blood-purity-1449
threads: T-WHITENING-PROJECT

[E-W1-024-01] W1 1565 land TB
Manila galleon trade route operational 1565-1815 (250 years)
